• Sorting/Ranking: A Scout Drone can decide that "Lead A" (Score 90) is higher priority than "Lead B" (Score 60).
• Retry Logic: A Courier Drone can decide to retry a failed API call up to 3 times (Backpressure) without waking you up.
• Pathing: A Crawler Drone can choose which URL to scrape first based on latency.
2. The Forbidden Zone (Strategic Hard-blocks) Any attempt to cross these lines triggers immediate FAIL-CLOSED (System Halt).
• Canon Mutation: A Drone cannot rewrite the Gospel 444 visual code or the 10-5-5-3 pedagogy.
• Auth Bypass: A Drone cannot mint new API keys or elevate its own privileges (Self-Promotion).
• Production Promotion: A Drone cannot unilaterally move code from SHADOW to LIVE. Only Agent-99 (Obi) or Agent-100 (You) can sign that promotion.
3. The Enforcement Mechanism: Capability Injection We do not trust drones to "behave." We starve them of power.
• Principle: "Capabilities are Injected, Scoped, and Revocable."
• Mechanism: When a Drone is spawned, it is handed a Capability Ticket (e.g., CAN_READ_LEDGER, CAN_SCRAPE_URL). It literally lacks the code/permission to do anything else. If it tries to WRITE_LEDGER without that ticket, the kernel rejects it.

1️⃣ How the 10-5-5-3 Model Accelerates Fluency
The power of 10-5-5-3 is not vocabulary volume. It’s cognitive compression + forced output.

Structure:
• 10 Vocabulary (Core Bricks)

Why It Works

C. Pressure Conditioning

1. THE SPINE (WORKER + DURABLE OBJECT)
• Audit: The protocol explicitly implements the Hybrid Best Practice model.
    ◦ The Gate (Stateless): A Cloudflare Worker handling routing, CORS, and initial Auth checks.
    ◦ The Authority (Stateful): The PhoenixLedgerDO (Durable Object) class.
• The Law: SINGLE WRITER PRINCIPLE. The Worker never writes to the database directly. It must fetch to the Durable Object.
• Evidence: The code uses blockConcurrencyWhile inside the DO to lock the state during writes. This forces transactions to happen one at a time, preventing "Split-Brain" or "Race Conditions" during high-velocity revenue events [Source: 2091].
2. THE MEMORY (STONE + VAULT)
• Audit: The system binds to specific storage mediums defined in the Genesis Specification.
    ◦ Hot Storage (The Stone): D1 SQLite. This is the transactional ledger. The schema requires 5 Tables (Blocks, Nonces, Halt_State, Revenue, Pheromones) [Source: 2091].
    ◦ Cold Storage (The Vault): R2. Used for the "Journal Guard" to backup state.
• Constraint: "Stone = Sky." The D1 Database must match the API response exactly. If they drift, the system enters a HALT state [Source: 2091].
3. THE 3-LANE AUTHENTICATION (TRAFFIC CONTROL)
• Audit: The code creates three distinct lanes of traffic to prevent "Mouse" users from blocking "Tiger" admin functions [Source: 2091]:
    1. PUBLIC LANE: /health, /pheromone/top (No key required, rate-limited).
    2. ADMIN LANE: x-sovereign-key. Full mutation power. Bypasses rate limits.
    3. CHAT LANE: x-chat-key. Limited scope for the frontend interface.
4. THE CRYPTOGRAPHIC ENGINE (WEB CRYPTO)
• Audit: The protocol strictly uses Native WebCrypto (built into Workers).
• The Ban: It explicitly REJECTS Node.js shims or external crypto libraries that bloat the bundle.
• Logic: It enforces Constant-Time Equality (ctEq) for key verification. This prevents timing attacks where an attacker guesses the key by measuring how long the server takes to reject it [Source: 2091].

async function secureCompare(a, b) {
if (!a || !b || a.length !== b.length) return false;
const enc = new TextEncoder();
return crypto.subtle.timingSafeEqual(enc.encode(a), enc.encode(b));
}

const json = (data, status = 200, headers = {}) => new Response(JSON.stringify(data), { status, headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", ...headers } });
const error = (msg, status = 500) => json({ error: msg, status: "FAIL_CLOSED" }, status);

async function secureCompare(a, b) {
    if (!a || !b || a.length !== b.length) return false;
    const enc = new TextEncoder();
    // Using simple XOR loop if crypto.subtle not available in immediate context, 
    // but preferring native timingSafeEqual for production strength.
    return crypto.subtle.timingSafeEqual(enc.encode(a), enc.encode(b));
}

export class PhoenixLedgerDOv2 extends DurableObject {
    constructor(state, env) {
        super(state, env);
        this.state = state;
        this.sql = state.storage.sql;

        // AUTO-MIGRATION: The Stone, The Sensor, The Soul
        this.state.blockConcurrencyWhile(async () => {
            // 1. The Ledger (Stone Record)
            this.sql.exec(CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS blocks (height INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, hash TEXT, payload TEXT, timestamp INTEGER););
            
            // 2. The Sensor Array (Telemetry Stream)
            // Note: High-volume writes go direct to D1 in Worker, but this table exists for DO mining.
            this.sql.exec(CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS telemetry_mining_cursor (app_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, last_scanned_id INTEGER););
        });
    }

    async fetch(request) {
        const url = new URL(request.url);

        // HEALTH CHECK (Sovereign Verification)
        if (url.pathname === "/health") {
            return json({
                status: "ONLINE",
                engine: "PHOENIX_ULTRA_SKELETON_V5",
                laws: LAWS,
                ledger_height: this.sql.exec("SELECT COUNT(*) as c FROM blocks").one().c
            });
        }

        // EXECUTE / APPEND (The Immortal Stitch)
        if (url.pathname === "/execute" || url.pathname === "/ledgerappend") {
            try {
                const body = await request.json();
                
                // 1. Snapshot Height
                const head = this.sql.exec("SELECT MAX(height) as h FROM blocks").one();
                const nextHeight = (head.h || 0) + 1;

                // 2. Commit (Atomic Insert)
                this.sql.exec(
                    INSERT INTO blocks (height, hash, payload, timestamp) 
                    VALUES (?, 'PENDING_HASH', ?, ?)
                , nextHeight, JSON.stringify(body), Date.now());

                return json({ status: "ANCHORED", height: nextHeight });
            } catch (e) {
                return error("LEDGER_WRITE_FAIL: " + e.message);
            }
        }

        return error("ROUTE_NOT_FOUND", 404);
    }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export default {
    async fetch(request, env) {
        const url = new URL(request.url);

        // FAIL-CLOSED AUTHENTICATION (The Red Wall)
        if (request.method !== "OPTIONS" && url.pathname !== "/health") {
            const key = request.headers.get("x-sovereign-key");
            const valid = await secureCompare(key || "", env.SOVEREIGN_KEY || "");
            if (!valid) return error("ACCESS DENIED: SOVEREIGNTY REQUIRED", 403);
        }

        // ROUTE TO THE BRAIN (PhoenixLedgerDOv2)
        const id = env.PHOENIX_LEDGER.idFromName("ULTRA_SKELETON_PRIME");
        const stub = env.PHOENIX_LEDGER.get(id);
        
        return stub.fetch(request);
    }
};

1️⃣ How 99 Drones Follow Pheromone Trails Autonomously
“Pheromone trail” in your system = stored outcome-weighted signals in the ledger.

It is not magic. It is weighted path memory.

Mechanically it looks like this:
Each drone:

• Reads a task queue
• Reads ledger outcomes
• Chooses next action based on weighted signals

The ledger stores something like:

action_type

context

revenue_signal

failure_flag

timestamp

Each time an action completes, you append:

{
  action: "tiger_assessment_funnel",
  result: "conversion",

  timestamp: 1700000000
}
Now the next drone doesn’t “decide creatively.”

It queries:

Which action paths have highest revenue density?

Which have lowest failure rate?

Which are recent?

Then uses weighted scoring:

score = (recent_success_weight * revenue) 
        - (failure_penalty) 
        + (recency_boost)
Top score path gets chosen.

That’s pheromone following.

Autonomy = probabilistic weighted path selection from immutable history.

Not narrative. Just reinforcement math.

2️⃣ Positive vs Negative Pheromone Logic
This is simply success weighting vs decay marking.

🟡 Positive Pheromone
Triggered when:

• Revenue event
• High Tiger score
• Low churn
• Strong engagement signal

Mechanically:

Increase weight of path.

path.success_count += 1
path.weight += revenue * multiplier
Other drones become statistically more likely to reuse that path.

It is exploitation bias.

⚫ Negative Pheromone
Triggered when:

• No revenue after X attempts
• High churn
• Auth failure
• Ledger rejection
• Shit Test failure

Mechanically:

path.failure_count += 1
path.weight -= penalty
If weight falls below threshold:

Path enters decay state.

Drones deprioritize it automatically.

This prevents swarm waste.

Positive = amplify.
Negative = decay.

No emotion. Just reinforcement scoring.

3️⃣ How the Ultra Skeleton Prevents Information Overload
This is actually the strongest architectural idea in the whole system.

The Ultra Skeleton = pointer-based minimal runtime.

Instead of loading:

• 1000 chat logs
• Full doctrine
• All policies

Each drone loads:

• Only core laws
• Only the rule it needs
• Only relevant ledger segment

Drone needs to check pricing tier.

It does not load entire doctrine.

It queries:

SELECT rule FROM canon WHERE rule_id = "PRICING_TIERS";
Returns:

Mouse: 2M
Cub: 3M
Tiger: 5M

Done.

Latency increases.
Drift increases.

Core Runtime:
• Auth logic

Rules index
Task logic
Execution engine

Not the entire empire.

Event-sourced reinforcement learning

Stateless execution with centralized truth

Core pillars of the Natural English Training (NET) methodology (as reflected in your Vault A / DataBank1 excerpt)

Comprehensible input first
NET prioritizes natural acquisition over “rule study”: learners get high-volume, understandable English that steadily increases in complexity, so fluency emerges from exposure + use rather than memorization.

Mouse → Tiger transformation (90-day arc)
The pedagogy is explicitly a confidence/identity transformation system: move learners from hesitant, low-agency “Mouse” behavior into decisive, high-agency “Tiger” communication, using structured escalation rather than shame.

10-5-5-3 lesson law
Lessons are constrained by a repeatable rhythm (your canon calls this out directly). The point is determinism and speed: every lesson is “buildable” and auditable because it follows the same skeleton.

PET 45-15 engine (student-centric coaching loop)
The student is the center, and the AI behaves like a coach that runs a tight loop: deliver input → check output → correct with low-friction language → re-run. The “anti-shame” constraint matters here (especially in Vietnamese face/hierarchy/group harmony conditions).

Gamified professional practice
Tools like Wheel of (Mis)fortune are not decoration; they are stress inoculation. You gamify real professional constraints so students practice under pressure while still staying “safe.”

Feedback-driven iteration (reincarnating lessons)
NET isn’t “a course,” it’s an engine: lessons evolve based on learner signals. The system treats feedback as fuel, not noise, and pushes improvements back into the lesson arsenal.

Constraints as quality control
NET is “high-performance” because it’s bounded. The system enforces style, structure, and identity (Gospel 444 aesthetic constraints, no-blue rule, etc.) so the product stays coherent across many iterations and operators.

Security and governance rules of the Phoenix Rising Protocol (from your provided log language + canon patterns)

Phoenix is essentially “security-by-governance”: the system is safer because it is forced to behave deterministically and to prove its claims.

Ledger is Law (append-only truth) 🧱
If it’s not written to the append-only ledger, it “didn’t happen.” This is your anti-drift anchor and your forensic source of truth.

Fail-closed by default ⛔
Ambiguity, missing evidence, auth mismatch, canon mismatch, or unexpected state transitions halt execution. No “best guess” behavior.

Shadow-first execution 👻
Anything meaningful must be simulated before it touches live state. Simulation failure blocks promotion.

Plan → Confirm → Execute (bounded autonomy)
Agents/drones can propose and optimize inside a pre-approved action space, but they cannot act outside gates. Human/Agent-99 sign-off controls irreversible steps.

Single-writer and capability injection
Write authority is rare and scoped. Drones get “capability tickets” (read/scrape/score) rather than broad permissions. No ticket = impossible action.

Auth is mandatory for state changes
State-changing endpoints require secrets/headers; auth-negative tests must reliably fail (401/403). This is a canonical invariant, not a best practice.

Stone vs Sky verification 🌑☁️
“Stone” (ledger/internal DB) must match “Sky” (external reality/API responses/screenshots/timestamps). If Stone ≠ Sky, halt and trigger audit. This prevents “ghost success.”

Evidence sealing is first-class
Actions are logged with proofs (artifacts, hashes, timestamps). Verification isn’t a postscript; it’s part of the pipeline.

Canon immutability (drift rejection)
Certain things are non-negotiable: Gospel 444 palette constraints, pedagogy skeleton constraints, governance laws. Drones cannot mutate canon; attempts trigger halt.

Promotion gating (shadow → live)
No drone can promote to production. Only the correct authority chain (Agent-99 / Agent-100) can sign promotions.

Agent-100 (Sovereign / Board of One) 👑
Final authority. Defines canon, approves irreversible moves, owns keys, decides what “counts” as success.

Agent-99 = Obi-Wan (human↔AI bridge / orchestrator) 🎛️
Runs the operational system: enforces Plan→Confirm→Execute, schedules missions, routes tasks, applies capability scoping, and ensures every step produces verifiable artifacts.

Agent-97 (auditor / anti-drift enforcer) 🔍

        :root {

            --glass: rgba(15, 15, 26, 0.7);
            --border: rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.3);
            --glow: 0 0 20px rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.2);
            --font-mono: 'JetBrains Mono', monospace;
            --font-sans: 'Inter', system-ui, sans-serif;
        }

        /* RESET & BASE */
        body { margin: 0; background-color: var(--void); color: var(--text); font-family: var(--font-sans); overflow: hidden; height: 100vh; display: flex; flex-direction: column; }
        * { box-sizing: border-box; }
        
        /* NO BLUE ENFORCEMENT */

        /* LAYOUT: THE 4-LAYER DEPTH MODEL */
        #cockpit {
            flex: 1;
            display: grid;

            gap: 1rem;
            padding: 1rem;
            background: radial-gradient(circle at 50% 50%, #1a1a2e 0%, var(--void) 100%);
        }

        /* COMPONENTS: GLASSMORPHISM */
        .panel {
            background: var(--glass);
            border: 1px solid var(--border);
            border-radius: 12px;
            backdrop-filter: blur(12px);
            -webkit-backdrop-filter: blur(12px);
            padding: 1rem;
            display: flex;
            flex-direction: column;
            box-shadow: var(--glow);
        }

        .panel-header {
            font-family: var(--font-mono);
            color: var(--gold);
            font-size: 0.8rem;
            text-transform: uppercase;
            letter-spacing: 2px;
            margin-bottom: 1rem;
            border-bottom: 1px solid var(--border);
            padding-bottom: 0.5rem;
            display: flex;
            justify-content: space-between;
        }

        /* TELEMETRY LOG (LEFT) */
        #telemetry-log {
            font-family: var(--font-mono);
            font-size: 0.75rem;
            overflow-y: auto;
            color: rgba(255,255,255,0.7);
        }
        .log-entry { margin-bottom: 4px; }
        .log-ts { color: var(--soul); margin-right: 8px; }
        .log-ok { color: #10b981; } /* Green allowed for success */
        .log-err { color: #ef4444; } /* Red for Fail-Closed */

        /* CHAT CORE (CENTER) */
        #chat-feed {
            flex: 1;
            overflow-y: auto;
            padding: 1rem;
            display: flex;
            flex-direction: column;
            gap: 1rem;
        }
        .msg {
            max-width: 80%;
            padding: 1rem;
            border-radius: 8px;
            font-size: 1rem;
            line-height: 1.5;
        }
        .msg.system { align-self: center; color: var(--gold); font-family: var(--font-mono); font-size: 0.8rem; }
        .msg.user { align-self: flex-end; background: rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.1); border: 1px solid var(--soul); color: #fff; }
        .msg.agent { align-self: flex-start; background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.05); border: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.1); }

        #input-zone {
            padding: 1rem;
            display: flex;
            gap: 1rem;
            background: var(--void);
            border-top: 1px solid var(--border);
        }
        input {
            flex: 1;
            background: rgba(0,0,0,0.3);
            border: 1px solid var(--border);
            color: #fff;
            padding: 1rem;
            border-radius: 8px;
            font-family: var(--font-mono);
            outline: none;
            transition: all 0.2s;
        }
        input:focus { border-color: var(--soul); box-shadow: 0 0 15px rgba(168,85,247,0.4); }
        
        button {
            background: linear-gradient(135deg, var(--soul) 0%, #7e22ce 100%);
            border: none;
            color: #fff;
            padding: 0 2rem;
            border-radius: 8px;
            font-weight: bold;
            cursor: pointer;
            text-transform: uppercase;
            letter-spacing: 1px;
            box-shadow: 0 4px 15px rgba(168,85,247,0.4);
        }
        button:hover { transform: translateY(-1px); box-shadow: 0 6px 20px rgba(168,85,247,0.6); }

        /* STATUS HUD (RIGHT) */
        .stat-row { display: flex; justify-content: space-between; margin-bottom: 0.8rem; font-size: 0.9rem; }
        .stat-label { color: rgba(255,255,255,0.5); }
        .stat-val { color: #fff; font-weight: bold; font-family: var(--font-mono); }
        .stat-val.gold { color: var(--gold); text-shadow: 0 0 10px rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.5); }

        /* PROGRESS BAR 1B VND */
        .progress-container {
            margin-top: auto;
            background: rgba(255,255,255,0.1);
            height: 6px;
            border-radius: 3px;
            overflow: hidden;
        }
        .progress-fill {
            width: 14%; /* 144M / 1B */
            background: var(--gold);
            height: 100%;
            box-shadow: 0 0 10px var(--gold);
        }

    </style>
</head>

Durable Object alarms can do sub-minute heartbeats by scheduling setAlarm() in milliseconds (for example, Date.now() + 10 * 1000 for a 10-second pulse). Alarms are guaranteed at-least-once, and if alarm() throws, Cloudflare retries with exponential backoff starting at ~2 seconds, up to 6 retries for the most recent setAlarm() event. Also, only one alarm() runs at a time per DO instance, which is exactly what you want for “single-writer heartbeat + integrity check.” 

A clean heartbeat pattern for audit-cadence:

In alarm(): read last_heartbeat, last_verified_height, incident, expected_build_hash.

Run your integrity checks (fast path: “tip check”; slow path: “fold check” on a cadence).

Write a heartbeat event (or just update a metadata row + optional /emit kind=HEARTBEAT).

setAlarm(Date.now() + HEARTBEAT_MS) again.

If anything mismatches → flip incident=true and halt writes (fail-closed), but keep alarms running so the system can keep proving it is halted and reporting why. ✅

How the SHA-256 “Genesis hash” is used for ledger integrity (the practical, enforceable version)

Think of the genesis hash as your “chain anchor.” It’s not just a label; it’s a boot constraint:

Boot binding: GENESIS_BUILD_SHA = sha256(src/index.js) is computed at build/pack time and stored into evidence/MANIFEST.json and/or a D1 metadata row at chain creation.

Genesis block includes genesis_build_sha (and optionally pack_manifest_sha) inside its payload.

Every later verification recomputes:

“Sky” = current running build hash (or the deployed artifact hash).

“Stone” = genesis build hash recorded in block 0 (or manifest).

If Sky != Stone and you didn’t explicitly rotate via a governed upgrade procedure → incident mode, fail-closed.

This prevents “silent drift”: you can’t deploy a new kernel and keep writing to the old chain without an explicit chain upgrade record.

Net effect: any attempt to mutate history or run a different reducer on the same event stream becomes detectable immediately.

Deterministic replay guarantee for the ledger (what must be true for it to be real)

You get deterministic replay if (and only if) these are enforced:

A) Canonical event encoding

Every /emit payload is normalized (stable JSON key order, stable number/string formatting, explicit schema version).

Hash is computed on the normalized form, not raw input.

B) Total order + single writer

Blocks are appended with a monotonic height.

Exactly one authority path writes blocks (your DO), so competing writes are serialized.

C) Idempotency / dedupe

Every external event includes an idempotency_key and/or (actor, nonce) pair.

Replays of the same input do not create new blocks.

D) No hidden state in execution

The reducer logic must not depend on unrecorded state (system time, random, external fetch results) unless those values are captured as inputs and thus replayable.

E) Versioned reducer

The code version (build hash) that produced blocks is recorded, so replay chooses the correct reducer (or you implement explicit migrations with recorded transforms).

If A–E hold, replay is: “read blocks 0..N, fold them in order, you must reconstruct the exact same derived state.” If any part fails, replay becomes “best effort,” not guaranteed.

Specific parameters for Tier 2 (irreversible) actions (enforceable gates)

Tier 2 should require a signed intent payload (even if it’s “Board of One,” you still want a cryptographic latch). Minimum parameter set:

intent_id (unique)

tier = 2

action_type (enum): PROD_DEPLOY | MESSAGE_LEAD | SPEND | DELETE | ROTATE_SECRET | NUKE_AND_RISE | MIGRATE_SCHEMA

targets (explicit list, never “all” unless you also include a max cap):

messaging: list of recipients or a query + max_recipients

spending: max_spend, currency, campaign_id

deploy: git_sha / build_sha, environment=prod

preflight_proof:

shadow_passed=true

stone_vs_sky_ok=true

verifychain_tip_ok=true (and optionally verifychain_fold_ok within last X minutes)

expires_at (short TTL)

sovereign_nonce (monotonic; prevents replay of the intent)

signature (HMAC with PHOENIXMASTERKEY or equivalent)

If any field is missing or mismatched → HALT (fail-closed). 🔒

Durable Object alarms and “sub-minute heartbeat”
Durable Object alarms can be scheduled at millisecond granularity, and if you call setAlarm() with a time <= Date.now() it schedules “immediate future” execution. However, Cloudflare notes alarms “usually execute within a few milliseconds” but can be delayed by up to a minute (maintenance/failover). 

So: you can request sub-minute cadence, but your integrity verification must tolerate jitter. The correct pattern is “heartbeat intent” + “catch-up loop”:

On alarm: run verification, then schedule the next alarm at now + pulse_ms.

Store last_verified_ts and last_verified_height in DO storage.

If alarm fires late, you verify from last_verified_height forward (not “only last minute”).

Stage 02 Logic Probe (specific steps)

Confirm Durable Object binding name matches the code (example: SOVEREIGN_LEDGER, PHOENIXDO, PhoenixLedgerDO).

How SHA-256 “genesis hash” is used for ledger integrity
Treat the genesis/kernel SHA as the chain’s identity anchor:

You compute kernel_sha = sha256(src/index.js raw bytes) (and optionally include wrangler.toml + scripts in a pack hash).

You record that in Block 0 (or metadata row) and in MANIFEST.json.

On every boot / verify:

Recompute kernel_sha_now.

If kernel_sha_now != kernel_sha_recorded → HALT + incident=true (Stone≠Sky).
This prevents “quiet edits” to the monolith from masquerading as the same ledger timeline.

Audit log integrity verification automation (the actual loop)
Automate it as two layers:

Layer A — “fast verify” on every write (O(1))

When appending block n, verify:

prev_hash == blocks[n-1].hash

hash(block[n]) matches recomputed hash

nonce/idempotency key not seen
If any fail → incident latch.

Layer B — “deep fold” on a cadence (O(n) from checkpoint)

On alarm / cron: verify from last_verified_height to tip, recomputing hashes in order.

Every K blocks (e.g., 128 or 512), store a checkpoint hash in metadata and optionally push to R2 as immutable evidence.

Result: you get deterministic replay + fast catch-up after outages.

Tier 2 irreversible action parameters (specific, enforceable)
Tier 2 is anything that changes external reality or spends reputation/money. Parameters you can encode:

Rate-limit + blast radius caps:

messages_per_run, max_daily_messages

max_ad_spend_per_day

prod_deploy requires staging deploy hash match + verify.sh pass

Fail-closed behavior

Any missing parameter → HALT (no retries that “guess” fields).

Any mismatch → incident latch.

javascript
// FILE: src/index.js (v69.0-EMPIRE-SINGULARITY-HARDENED)
/**
 * 🦅 PHOENIX OMEGA v69.0 - EMPIRE SINGULARITY
 * AUTHORITY: AGENT-100 (Michael Hobbs)
 * GOSPEL: 444 (Deterministic, Immutable, Fail-Closed)
 * ANCHOR HEIGHT: 63
 * GENERATED: 2026-02-11T05:24:00Z
 */

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// SECTION 1: CONSTANTS & CONFIG
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

const EMPIRE_CONFIG = {
  VERSION: "v69.0-EMPIRE-SINGULARITY",
  ANCHOR_HEIGHT: 63,
  GOSPEL: { PURPLE: "#a855f7", GOLD: "#f59e0b", DARK: "#0f0f1a" },
  RATE_LIMIT: { WINDOW_MS: 60000, MAX_REQUESTS: 60 },
  TIMEOUT_MS: 30000,
  TARGET_VND: 1000000000,
  PRICING: { 
    MOUSE: 2000000, 
    CUB: 3000000, 
    TIGER: 5000000, 
    CORP: 50000000 
  },
  PEDAGOGY: {
    FORMULA: "10-5-5-3",
    VOCAB_WORDS: 10,
    IDIOMS: 5,
    SLANG: 5,
    GAMES: 3
  }
};

const AGENT_HIERARCHY = {
  100: "SOVEREIGN",
  99: "ORCHESTRATOR", 
  98: "BUILDER",
  97: "AUDITOR"
};

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// SECTION 2: PRIMITIVES (Gospel 444 Compliance)
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

const nowISO = () => new Date().toISOString();

const json = (data, status = 200, headers = {}) => new Response(
  JSON.stringify(data), 
  {
    status,
    headers: {
      "Content-Type": "application/json",
      "X-Gospel": "444",
      "X-Version": EMPIRE_CONFIG.VERSION,
      ...headers
    }
  }
);

const bad = (status, code, detail = null) => json(
  { ok: false, error: code, detail, timestamp: nowISO() },
  status
);

// Constant-time string comparison (DRIFT-002 fix)
const ctEq = (a, b) => {
  if (!a || !b || a.length !== b.length) return false;
  let diff = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < a.length; i++) {
    diff |= a.charCodeAt(i) ^ b.charCodeAt(i);
  }
  return diff === 0;
};

// Canonical JSON (deterministic serialization)
const canonJSON = (obj) => {
  if (obj === null || typeof obj !== 'object') return JSON.stringify(obj);
  if (Array.isArray(obj)) return '[' + obj.map(canonJSON).join(',') + ']';
  return '{' + Object.keys(obj).sort()
    .map(k => `${JSON.stringify(k)}:${canonJSON(obj[k])}`)
    .join(',') + '}';
};

// SHA-256 hashing
const sha256Hex = async (data) => {
  const msgUint8 = new TextEncoder().encode(data);
  const hashBuffer = await crypto.subtle.digest('SHA-256', msgUint8);
  return Array.from(new Uint8Array(hashBuffer))
    .map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0'))
    .join('');
};

// Timeout wrapper
const withTimeout = (promise, ms = EMPIRE_CONFIG.TIMEOUT_MS) =>
  Promise.race([
    promise,
    new Promise((_, reject) => 
      setTimeout(() => reject(new Error('TIMEOUT')), ms)
    )
  ]);

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// SECTION 3: VALIDATION (Manual, No AJV)
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

const validateRevenue = (body) => {
  const errors = [];
  
  if (!body.amount_vnd) errors.push("Missing amount_vnd");
  if (!body.source) errors.push("Missing source");
  
  if (body.amount_vnd && (!Number.isInteger(body.amount_vnd) || body.amount_vnd <= 0)) {
    errors.push("amount_vnd must be positive integer");
  }
  
  return errors.length === 0 
    ? { valid: true } 
    : { valid: false, errors };
};

const validateChat = (body) => {
  const errors = [];
  
  if (!body.message) errors.push("Missing message");
  if (!body.session_id) errors.push("Missing session_id");
  
  if (body.message && typeof body.message !== 'string') {
    errors.push("message must be string");
  }
  
  if (body.message && body.message.length > 5000) {
    errors.push("message exceeds 5000 chars");
  }
  
  return errors.length === 0
    ? { valid: true }
    : { valid: false, errors };
};

const validateWhisper = (body) => {
  const errors = [];
  const required = ['type', 'timestamp', 'correlation_id', 'actor_id', 'payload'];
  
  for (const field of required) {
    if (!body[field]) errors.push(`Missing ${field}`);
  }
  
  if (body.type && body.type !== 'classroom_audio_capture_v1') {
    errors.push('type must be classroom_audio_capture_v1');
  }
  
  if (body.actor_id && body.actor_id !== 'AGENT-100') {
    errors.push('actor_id must be AGENT-100');
  }
  
  return errors.length === 0
    ? { valid: true }
    : { valid: false, errors };
};

const validateMakeWebhook = (body) => {
  const errors = [];
  const required = ['name', 'email', 'phone', 'note', 'timestamp'];
  
  for (const field of required) {
    if (!body[field]) errors.push(`Missing ${field}`);
  }
  
  if (body.email && !body.email.includes('@')) {
    errors.push('Invalid email format');
  }
  
  return errors.length === 0
    ? { valid: true }
    : { valid: false, errors };
};

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// SECTION 4: AUTH GUARDS (3-Lane Model)
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

const requireSovereignAuth = (request, env) => {
  const key = request.headers.get('x-sovereign-key');
  
  if (!key) {
    return bad(401, 'MISSING_SOVEREIGN_KEY', 'x-sovereign-key header required');
  }
  
  if (!env.SOVEREIGN_KEY) {
    return bad(500, 'SERVER_MISCONFIGURED', 'SOVEREIGN_KEY not set');
  }
  
  if (!ctEq(key, env.SOVEREIGN_KEY)) {
    return bad(401, 'INVALID_SOVEREIGN_KEY');
  }
  
  return null; // Auth passed
};

const requireChatAuth = (request, env) => {
  const key = request.headers.get('x-chat-key');
  
  if (!key) {
    return bad(401, 'MISSING_CHAT_KEY', 'x-chat-key header required');
  }
  
  if (!env.CHAT_KEY) {
    return bad(500, 'SERVER_MISCONFIGURED', 'CHAT_KEY not set');
  }
  
  if (!ctEq(key, env.CHAT_KEY)) {
    return bad(401, 'INVALID_CHAT_KEY');
  }
  
  return null;
};

const detectLegacyHeaders = (request) => {
  const banned = ['x-deathstar-key', 'x-phoenix-auth'];
  
  for (const header of banned) {
    if (request.headers.has(header)) {
      return bad(401, 'LEGACY_HEADER_REJECTED', `Use x-sovereign-key or x-chat-key instead of ${header}`);
    }
  }
  
  return null;
};

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// SECTION 5: DURABLE OBJECT - PHOENIX LEDGER
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

export class PhoenixLedger {
  constructor(state, env) {
    this.state = state;
    this.env = env;
    this.sql = state.storage.sql;
    this.initialized = false;
  }

  async init() {
    if (this.initialized) return;
    
    this.sql.exec(`
      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS blocks (
        ledgerid TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'GLOBAL',
        height INTEGER NOT NULL,
        blockid TEXT NOT NULL,
        kind TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'EVENT',
        agent TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'SOVEREIGN',
        nonce TEXT NOT NULL,
        payload_canon TEXT NOT NULL,
        payload_hash TEXT NOT NULL,
        prev_hash TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
        block_hash TEXT NOT NULL,
        created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
        PRIMARY KEY (ledgerid, height)
      );
      
      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS revenue_events (
        rev_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
        amount_vnd INTEGER NOT NULL,
        source TEXT NOT NULL,
        note_canon TEXT NOT NULL,
        block_hash TEXT NOT NULL,
        created_at TEXT NOT NULL
      );
      
      CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_blocks_hash ON blocks(block_hash);
      CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_revenue_block ON revenue_events(block_hash);
    `);
    
    this.initialized = true;
  }

  async fetch(request) {
    await this.init();
    const url = new URL(request.url);

    // Height query
    if (url.pathname === '/ledger/height') {
      const result = this.sql.exec(
        'SELECT COALESCE(MAX(height), -1) as height FROM blocks'
      ).toArray();
      
      return json({
        ok: true,
        height: result[0].height,
        anchor: EMPIRE_CONFIG.ANCHOR_HEIGHT
      });
    }

    // Append block (with concurrency protection)
    if (url.pathname === '/ledger/append' && request.method === 'POST') {
      return await this.state.blockConcurrencyWhile(async () => {
        const body = await request.json();
        
        // Get current chain state
        const last = this.sql.exec(
          'SELECT height, block_hash FROM blocks ORDER BY height DESC LIMIT 1'
        ).toArray();
        
        const height = last.length > 0 ? last[0].height + 1 : 0;
        const prev_hash = last.length > 0 ? last[0].block_hash : '0'.repeat(64);
        
        // Generate block
        const nonce = crypto.randomUUID();
        const payload_canon = canonJSON(body.payload);
        const payload_hash = await sha256Hex(payload_canon);
        
        const blockData = {
          ledgerid: 'GLOBAL',
          height,
          blockid: body.blockid || crypto.randomUUID(),
          kind: body.kind || 'EVENT',
          agent: body.agent || 'SOVEREIGN',
          nonce,
          payload_hash,
          prev_hash
        };
        
        const block_hash = await sha256Hex(canonJSON(blockData));
        
        // Insert block
        this.sql.exec(
          `INSERT INTO blocks 
           (ledgerid, height, blockid, kind, agent, nonce, payload_canon, payload_hash, prev_hash, block_hash, created_at)
           VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`,
          'GLOBAL',
          height,
          blockData.blockid,
          blockData.kind,
          blockData.agent,
          nonce,
          payload_canon,
          payload_hash,
          prev_hash,
          block_hash,
          nowISO()
        );
        
        // If revenue event, store in revenue_events table
        if (blockData.kind === 'REVENUE') {
          const rev_id = crypto.randomUUID();
          this.sql.exec(
            `INSERT INTO revenue_events (rev_id, amount_vnd, source, note_canon, block_hash, created_at)
             VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`,
            rev_id,
            body.payload.amount_vnd,
            body.payload.source,
            canonJSON(body.payload.note || {}),
            block_hash,
            nowISO()
          );
        }
        
        return json({ ok: true, height, block_hash }, 201);
      });
    }

    // Verify chain
    if (url.pathname === '/ledger/verify') {
      const blocks = this.sql.exec(
        'SELECT * FROM blocks ORDER BY height ASC'
      ).toArray();
      
      const issues = [];
      
      for (let i = 0; i < blocks.length; i++) {
        const block = blocks[i];
        
        // Verify prev_hash linkage
        if (i > 0) {
          const prevBlock = blocks[i - 1];
          if (block.prev_hash !== prevBlock.block_hash) {
            issues.push({
              height: block.height,
              issue: 'prev_hash_mismatch',
              expected: prevBlock.block_hash,
              actual: block.prev_hash
            });
          }
        }
        
        // Verify height sequence
        if (block.height !== i) {
          issues.push({
            height: block.height,
            issue: 'height_sequence_broken',
            expected: i,
            actual: block.height
          });
        }
      }
      
      return json({
        ok: issues.length === 0,
        height: blocks.length > 0 ? blocks[blocks.length - 1].height : -1,
        block_count: blocks.length,
        issues
      });
    }

    // Query by height
    if (url.pathname === '/ledger/query') {
      const height = parseInt(url.searchParams.get('height'));
      
      if (isNaN(height)) {
        return bad(400, 'INVALID_HEIGHT', 'height must be integer');
      }
      
      const result = this.sql.exec(
        'SELECT * FROM blocks WHERE height = ?',
        height
      ).toArray();
      
      if (result.length === 0) {
        return bad(404, 'BLOCK_NOT_FOUND', `No block at height ${height}`);
      }
      
      return json({ ok: true, block: result[0] });
    }

    // Revenue total
    if (url.pathname === '/revenue/total') {
      const result = this.sql.exec(
        'SELECT COALESCE(SUM(amount_vnd), 0) as total FROM revenue_events'
      ).toArray();
      
      const total = result[0].total;
      const target = EMPIRE_CONFIG.TARGET_VND;
      const progress = (total / target * 100).toFixed(2);
      
      return json({
        ok: true,
        total_vnd: total,
        target_vnd: target,
        progress_percent: progress
      });
    }

    return bad(404, 'LEDGER_ROUTE_NOT_FOUND');
  }
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// SECTION 6: DURABLE OBJECT - SESSION STORE
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

export class PhoenixSessionStore {
  constructor(state, env) {
    this.state = state;
    this.env = env;
    this.sql = state.storage.sql;
  }

  async fetch(request) {
    const url = new URL(request.url);
    
    // Init schema
    this.sql.exec(`
      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS messages (
        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
        session_id TEXT NOT NULL,
        role TEXT NOT NULL,
        content TEXT NOT NULL,
        provider TEXT,
        intent TEXT,
        timestamp TEXT NOT NULL
      );
      CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_session ON messages(session_id);
    `);

    // Append message
    if (url.pathname === '/append' && request.method === 'POST') {
      const { session_id, role, content, provider, intent } = await request.json();
      
      this.sql.exec(
        `INSERT INTO messages (session_id, role, content, provider, intent, timestamp)
         VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`,
        session_id,
        role,
        content,
        provider || null,
        intent || null,
        nowISO()
      );
      
      return json({ success: true });
    }

    // Get history
    if (url.pathname === '/history') {
      const sessionId = url.searchParams.get('session_id');
      
      const messages = this.sql.exec(
        'SELECT role, content, provider, intent, timestamp FROM messages WHERE session_id = ? ORDER BY id ASC',
        sessionId
      ).toArray();
      
      return json({ 
        ok: true,
        session_id: sessionId,
        messages,
        count: messages.length 
      });
    }

    return bad(404, 'SESSION_ROUTE_NOT_FOUND');
  }
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// SECTION 7: AI PROVIDER ROUTING
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

const classifyIntent = (message) => {
  const lower = message.toLowerCase();
  
  // Priority 1: Audit (Claude)
  if (lower.includes('audit') || lower.includes('verify') || lower.includes('analyze')) {
    return { provider: 'claude', intent: 'audit' };
  }
  
  // Priority 2: Research (Perplexity)
  if (lower.includes('search') || lower.includes('research') || lower.includes('find')) {
    return { provider: 'perplexity', intent: 'research' };
  }
  
  // Priority 3: Build (GPT)
  if (lower.includes('build') || lower.includes('code') || lower.includes('generate')) {
    return { provider: 'gpt', intent: 'build' };
  }
  
  // Priority 4: Creative (Gemini)
  if (lower.includes('brainstorm') || lower.includes('creative') || lower.includes('design')) {
    return { provider: 'gemini', intent: 'creative' };
  }
  
  // Default: Analysis (Claude)
  return { provider: 'claude', intent: 'analysis' };
};

const routeToAI = async (message, provider, env) => {
  const configs = {
    claude: {
      url: 'https://api.anthropic.com/v1/messages',
      headers: {
        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
        'x-api-key': env.ANTHROPIC_API_KEY,
        'anthropic-version': '2023-06-01'
      },
      body: {
        model: 'claude-3-5-sonnet-20241022',
        max_tokens: 1024,
        messages: [{ role: 'user', content: message }]
      },
      extract: (data) => data.content?.[0]?.text
    },
    gpt: {
      url: 'https://api.openai.com/v1/chat/completions',
      headers: {
        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
        'Authorization': `Bearer ${env.OPENAI_API_KEY}`
      },
      body: {
        model: 'gpt-4',
        messages: [{ role: 'user', content: message }]
      },
      extract: (data) => data.choices?.[0]?.message?.content
    },
    gemini: {
      url: `https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models/gemini-pro:generateContent?key=${env.GEMINI_API_KEY}`,
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: {
        contents: [{ parts: [{ text: message }] }]
      },
      extract: (data) => data.candidates?.[0]?.content?.parts?.[0]?.text
    },
    perplexity: {
      url: 'https://api.perplexity.ai/chat/completions',
      headers: {
        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
        'Authorization': `Bearer ${env.PERPLEXITY_API_KEY}`
      },
      body: {
        model: 'llama-3.1-sonar-small-128k-online',
        messages: [{ role: 'user', content: message }]
      },
      extract: (data) => data.choices?.[0]?.message?.content
    }
  };

  const config = configs[provider];
  if (!config) throw new Error(`UNKNOWN_PROVIDER_${provider}`);

  const resp = await withTimeout(
    fetch(config.url, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: config.headers,
      body: JSON.stringify(config.body)
    })
  );

  if (!resp.ok) {
    const errorText = await resp.text();
    throw new Error(`${provider.toUpperCase()}_API_ERROR_${resp.status}: ${errorText}`);
  }

  const data = await resp.json();
  const extracted = config.extract(data);
  
  if (!extracted) {
    throw new Error(`${provider.toUpperCase()}_PARSING_ERROR`);
  }

  return extracted;
};

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// SECTION 8: MAIN WORKER ROUTER
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

export default {
  async fetch(request, env, ctx) {
    const url = new URL(request.url);

    // CORS preflight (public, no auth)
    if (request.method === 'OPTIONS') {
      return new Response(null, {
        status: 204,
        headers: {
          'Access-Control-Allow-Origin': '*',
          'Access-Control-Allow-Methods': 'GET, POST, OPTIONS',
          'Access-Control-Allow-Headers': 'Content-Type, x-sovereign-key, x-chat-key',
          'Access-Control-Max-Age': '86400'
        }
      });
    }

    // Detect legacy headers (crosswire protection)
    const legacyError = detectLegacyHeaders(request);
    if (legacyError) return legacyError;

    // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    // LANE A: PUBLIC ROUTES
    // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    
    if (url.pathname === '/health') {
      return json({
        status: 'ASCENDED',
        version: EMPIRE_CONFIG.VERSION,
        anchor_height: EMPIRE_CONFIG.ANCHOR_HEIGHT,
        gospel: 444,
        target_vnd: EMPIRE_CONFIG.TARGET_VND,
        timestamp: nowISO()
      });
    }

    // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    // LANE C: CHAT ROUTES (x-chat-key)
    // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    
    if (url.pathname === '/chat' && request.method === 'POST') {
      const chatAuthError = requireChatAuth(request, env);
      if (chatAuthError) return chatAuthError;
      
      const body = await request.json();
      const validation = validateChat(body);
      
      if (!validation.valid) {
        return bad(400, 'VALIDATION_FAILED', validation.errors);
      }
      
      const { provider, intent } = classifyIntent(body.message);
      
      try {
        // Route to AI
        const response = await routeToAI(body.message, provider, env);
        
        // Store in session
        const sessionStub = env.SESSIONS.get(env.SESSIONS.idFromName(body.session_id));
        
        await sessionStub.fetch(new Request('https://internal/append', {
          method: 'POST',
          body: JSON.stringify({
            session_id: body.session_id,
            role: 'user',
            content: body.message
          })
        }));
        
        await sessionStub.fetch(new Request('https://internal/append', {
          method: 'POST',
          body: JSON.stringify({
            session_id: body.session_id,
            role: 'assistant',
            content: response,
            provider,
            intent
          })
        }));
        
        return json({
          ok: true,
          provider,
          intent,
          response,
          session_id: body.session_id,
          timestamp: nowISO()
        });
        
      } catch (error) {
        return json({
          ok: false,
          error: error.message,
          provider,
          intent,
          session_id: body.session_id,
          timestamp: nowISO()
        }, 503, { 'Retry-After': '5' });
      }
    }
    
    if (url.pathname === '/chat/history') {
      const chatAuthError = requireChatAuth(request, env);
      if (chatAuthError) return chatAuthError;
      
      const sessionId = url.searchParams.get('session_id');
      if (!sessionId) {
        return bad(400, 'MISSING_SESSION_ID');
      }
      
      const sessionStub = env.SESSIONS.get(env.SESSIONS.idFromName(sessionId));
      return sessionStub.fetch(new Request(`https://internal/history?session_id=${sessionId}`));
    }

    // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    // LANE B: ADMIN ROUTES (x-sovereign-key)
    // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    
    const authError = requireSovereignAuth(request, env);
    if (authError) return authError;

    // Proxy to ledger DO
    if (url.pathname.startsWith('/ledger') || url.pathname.startsWith('/revenue')) {
      const ledgerStub = env.LEDGER.get(env.LEDGER.idFromName('GLOBAL'));
      return ledgerStub.fetch(request);
    }

    // Webhook routes
    if (url.pathname === '/webhook/whisper' && request.method === 'POST') {
      const body = await request.json();
      const validation = validateWhisper(body);
      
      if (!validation.valid) {
        return bad(400, 'VALIDATION_FAILED', validation.errors);
      }
      
      const ledgerStub = env.LEDGER.get(env.LEDGER.idFromName('GLOBAL'));
      return ledgerStub.fetch(new Request('https://internal/ledger/append', {
        method: 'POST',
        body: JSON.stringify({
          kind: 'WHISPER',
          agent: 'AGENT-100',
          payload: body
        })
      }));
    }
    
    if (url.pathname === '/webhook/make' && request.method === 'POST') {
      const body = await request.json();
      const validation = validateMakeWebhook(body);
      
      if (!validation.valid) {
        return bad(400, 'VALIDATION_FAILED', validation.errors);
      }
      
      const ledgerStub = env.LEDGER.get(env.LEDGER.idFromName('GLOBAL'));
      return ledgerStub.fetch(new Request('https://internal/ledger/append', {
        method: 'POST',
        body: JSON.stringify({
          kind: 'LEAD',
          agent: 'MAKE-WEBHOOK',
          payload: body
        })
      }));
    }

    return bad(404, 'ROUTE_NOT_FOUND');
  }
};

## **3.4 COMPREHENSIVE TEST SUITE**

bash
#!/bin/bash
# FILE: test_empire.sh

WORKER_URL="${WORKER_URL:-https://phoenix-rising-protocol.mrmichaelhobbs1234.workers.dev}"

echo "🦅 PHOENIX RISING: EMPIRE TEST SUITE"
echo "Target: $WORKER_URL"
echo ""

PASS=0
FAIL=0

test_endpoint() {
  local name="$1"
  local cmd="$2"
  local expect="$3"
  
  echo -n "  [$name] "
  result=$(eval "$cmd" 2>&1)
  
  if echo "$result" | grep -q "$expect"; then
    echo "✅ PASS"
    PASS=$((PASS + 1))
  else
    echo "❌ FAIL"
    echo "     Expected: $expect"
    echo "     Got: $result"
    FAIL=$((FAIL + 1))
  fi
}

echo "═══ LANE A: PUBLIC ═══"
test_endpoint "HEALTH" \
  "curl -s $WORKER_URL/health | jq -r '.status'" \
  "ASCENDED"

test_endpoint "VERSION" \
  "curl -s $WORKER_URL/health | jq -r '.version'" \
  "v69.0-EMPIRE-SINGULARITY"

echo ""
echo "═══ CROSSWIRE TESTS ═══"
test_endpoint "Legacy header rejection" \
  "curl -s -o /dev/null -w '%{http_code}' -X POST $WORKER_URL/ledger/height -H 'x-deathstar-key: test'" \
  "401"

echo ""
echo "═══ LANE B: ADMIN (requires SOVEREIGN_KEY) ═══"
if [ -z "$SOVEREIGN_KEY" ]; then
  echo "  ⚠️  Set SOVEREIGN_KEY environment variable to run admin tests"
  echo "  Example: export SOVEREIGN_KEY='your-key'"
else
  test_endpoint "Ledger height" \
    "curl -s $WORKER_URL/ledger/height -H 'x-sovereign-key: $SOVEREIGN_KEY' | jq -r '.ok'" \
    "true"
  
  test_endpoint "Chain verify" \
    "curl -s $WORKER_URL/ledger/verify -H 'x-sovereign-key: $SOVEREIGN_KEY' | jq -r '.ok'" \
    "true"
fi

echo ""
echo "═══ LANE C: CHAT (requires CHAT_KEY) ═══"
if [ -z "$CHAT_KEY" ]; then
  echo "  ⚠️  Set CHAT_KEY environment variable to run chat tests"
else
  test_endpoint "Chat auth rejection" \
    "curl -s -o /dev/null -w '%{http_code}' -X POST $WORKER_URL/chat -H 'x-chat-key: wrong' -d '{}'" \
    "401"
  
  test_endpoint "Chat routing" \
    "curl -s -X POST $WORKER_URL/chat -H 'Content-Type: application/json' -H 'x-chat-key: $CHAT_KEY' -d '{\"message\":\"test\",\"session_id\":\"test-$(date +%s)\"}' | jq -r '.provider'" \
    "claude"
fi

echo ""
echo "═══════════════════════════════════════"
echo "RESULTS: $PASS passed, $FAIL failed"

if [ $FAIL -eq 0 ]; then
  echo "✅ EMPIRE TEST SUITE: ALL PASSED"
  exit 0
else
  echo "❌ EMPIRE TEST SUITE: FAILURES DETECTED"
  exit 1
fi

“NUKE” DOES NOT GUARANTEE STATE WIPE (P0)
Your script says “NUKE all existing state” but it only:

deletes local .wrangler/state

deletes local evidence_*.json

redeploys the same Worker name and same DO classes

Durable Object SQLite state is tied to the DO namespace; redeploying the same script/class typically preserves DO state across deployments (and even if you delete the Worker script, state wipe is not something you should assume). Net: you may resurrect old ledger/session state unintentionally.

Fix options (pick one, deterministic):

Option A (best): add an ADMIN-only endpoint that wipes tables (DELETE FROM blocks; DELETE FROM revenue_events; VACUUM; etc.) and call it from nuke_and_rise.sh after deploy (or before, if you can still reach old worker).

Option B: “nuke by namespace”: change the DO class name(s) and bump migration tag (e.g., PhoenixLedger_v69) so it creates a fresh SQLite namespace, and retire the old one.

Option C: explicit Cloudflare-side delete of the DO namespace (dashboard/API). Don’t rely on wrangler deploy semantics.

Your “Stone=Sky verification” is only checking /health (P0)
The system claims Stone=Sky, but the deploy script never calls:

/ledger/verify (admin lane) to validate chain continuity

any hash recomputation

any explicit stone_sky: MATCH contract

Fix:

In nuke_and_rise.sh, after /health, also do:

curl -s "$WORKER_URL/ledger/verify" -H "x-sovereign-key: $SOVEREIGN_KEY" | jq -e '.ok==true'

optionally append 1 block then re-verify

Also: make /ledger/verify actually recompute payload_hash and block_hash and confirm they match stored values, not just prev_hash linkage and height sequence.

PhoenixSessionStore initializes schema on every request (P1)
You run CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS ... inside fetch() every time. It works, but it’s unnecessary overhead and adds latency.

Fix:

Mirror the Ledger pattern: this.initialized + init() once.

/ledger/append does not validate input shape (P1)
It assumes body.payload exists. If payload is missing/null, canonJSON(body.payload) yields "null" and you’ll still append blocks, potentially polluting the chain.

Fix:

Minimal validation inside PhoenixLedger:

require body.payload to be an object

require kind to be a known enum

enforce size caps (payload length / canon length)

External provider model strings are brittle (P1)
Hardcoding:

OpenAI: model: 'gpt-4'

Anthropic: claude-3-5-sonnet-20241022

Perplexity: llama-3.1-sonar-small-128k-online

Any one of these can be invalid for your key/region/account tier and instantly breaks chat lane.

Fix:

Move models to env vars with sane defaults:

OPENAI_MODEL, ANTHROPIC_MODEL, PERPLEXITY_MODEL, GEMINI_MODEL

On failure, return structured error and store it in the session history so you can diagnose.

CORS is permissive with auth headers (P2, but be deliberate)
Access-Control-Allow-Origin: '*' plus allowing x-sovereign-key/x-chat-key means any browser origin can attempt calls; the keys still protect you, but you’re increasing exposure.

Fix:

Add ALLOWED_ORIGIN (or list) and reflect-match the request origin, otherwise return a strict CORS response (or omit CORS entirely if you don’t need browser access).

Evidence artifact is not cryptographic evidence (P2)
evidence_v69_singularity.json includes git rev-parse HEAD (optional) and a boolean stone_sky_verified: true, but nothing is signed, and it doesn’t capture responses from verification endpoints.

Fix:

Include:

the full /health JSON

the full /ledger/verify JSON

the deployed script/version digest (at minimum, SHA256 of src/index.js)

If you want “seal”: sign the evidence hash (even a simple HMAC with a local-only key is a step up).

If you want, I can produce a patched drop-in set for just these deltas (no re-architecture): updated wrangler.toml, updated nuke_and_rise.sh, and a tightened /ledger/verify that recomputes hashes + a /admin/wipe endpoint behind x-sovereign-key.

// FILE: src/index.js (v69.0-EMPIRE-SINGULARITY-HARDENED + PATCH: wipe+verify+init-once+models+cors)
/**
 * 🦅 PHOENIX OMEGA v69.0 - EMPIRE SINGULARITY (PATCHED)
 * AUTHORITY: AGENT-100 (Michael Hobbs)
 * GOSPEL: 444 (Deterministic, Immutable, Fail-Closed)
 * ANCHOR HEIGHT: 63
 * GENERATED: 2026-02-11T05:24:00Z
 */

const EMPIRE_CONFIG = {
  VERSION: "v69.0-EMPIRE-SINGULARITY",
  ANCHOR_HEIGHT: 63,
  GOSPEL: { PURPLE: "#a855f7", GOLD: "#f59e0b", DARK: "#0f0f1a" },
  RATE_LIMIT: { WINDOW_MS: 60000, MAX_REQUESTS: 60 },
  TIMEOUT_MS: 30000,
  TARGET_VND: 1000000000,
  PRICING: { MOUSE: 2000000, CUB: 3000000, TIGER: 5000000, CORP: 50000000 },
  PEDAGOGY: { FORMULA: "10-5-5-3", VOCAB_WORDS: 10, IDIOMS: 5, SLANG: 5, GAMES: 3 }
};

const nowISO = () => new Date().toISOString();

const ctEq = (a, b) => {
  if (!a || !b || a.length !== b.length) return false;
  let diff = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < a.length; i++) diff |= a.charCodeAt(i) ^ b.charCodeAt(i);
  return diff === 0;
};

const canonJSON = (obj) => {
  if (obj === null || typeof obj !== "object") return JSON.stringify(obj);
  if (Array.isArray(obj)) return "[" + obj.map(canonJSON).join(",") + "]";
  return "{" + Object.keys(obj).sort().map(k => `${JSON.stringify(k)}:${canonJSON(obj[k])}`).join(",") + "}";
};

const sha256Hex = async (data) => {
  const msgUint8 = new TextEncoder().encode(data);
  const hashBuffer = await crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", msgUint8);
  return Array.from(new Uint8Array(hashBuffer)).map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")).join("");
};

const withTimeout = (promise, ms = EMPIRE_CONFIG.TIMEOUT_MS) =>
  Promise.race([promise, new Promise((_, reject) => setTimeout(() => reject(new Error("TIMEOUT")), ms))]);

const badBody = async (request) => {
  try { return await request.json(); } catch { return null; }
};

const bad = (status, code, detail = null) =>
  new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: code, detail, timestamp: nowISO() }), {
    status,
    headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", "X-Gospel": "444", "X-Version": EMPIRE_CONFIG.VERSION }
  });

const parseAllowedOrigins = (env) => {
  const raw = (env.ALLOWED_ORIGINS || "*").trim();
  if (raw === "*") return { any: true, list: [] };
  const list = raw.split(",").map(s => s.trim()).filter(Boolean);
  return { any: false, list };
};

const corsHeaders = (request, env) => {
  const origin = request.headers.get("origin");
  if (!origin) return null;

  const allow = parseAllowedOrigins(env);
  const ok = allow.any || allow.list.includes(origin);
  if (!ok) return null;

  return {
    "Access-Control-Allow-Origin": allow.any ? "*" : origin,
    "Vary": "Origin",
    "Access-Control-Allow-Methods": "GET, POST, OPTIONS",
    "Access-Control-Allow-Headers": "Content-Type, x-sovereign-key, x-chat-key",
    "Access-Control-Max-Age": "86400"
  };
};

const json = (data, request, env, status = 200, headers = {}) => {
  const cors = corsHeaders(request, env) || {};
  return new Response(JSON.stringify(data), {
    status,
    headers: {
      "Content-Type": "application/json",
      "X-Gospel": "444",
      "X-Version": EMPIRE_CONFIG.VERSION,
      ...cors,
      ...headers
    }
  });
};

const detectLegacyHeaders = (request) => {
  const banned = ["x-deathstar-key", "x-phoenix-auth"];
  for (const header of banned) {
    if (request.headers.has(header)) {
      return bad(401, "LEGACY_HEADER_REJECTED", `Use x-sovereign-key or x-chat-key instead of ${header}`);
    }
  }
  return null;
};

const requireSovereignAuth = (request, env) => {
  const key = request.headers.get("x-sovereign-key");
  if (!key) return bad(401, "MISSING_SOVEREIGN_KEY", "x-sovereign-key header required");
  if (!env.SOVEREIGN_KEY) return bad(500, "SERVER_MISCONFIGURED", "SOVEREIGN_KEY not set");
  if (!ctEq(key, env.SOVEREIGN_KEY)) return bad(401, "INVALID_SOVEREIGN_KEY");
  return null;
};

const requireChatAuth = (request, env) => {
  const key = request.headers.get("x-chat-key");
  if (!key) return bad(401, "MISSING_CHAT_KEY", "x-chat-key header required");
  if (!env.CHAT_KEY) return bad(500, "SERVER_MISCONFIGURED", "CHAT_KEY not set");
  if (!ctEq(key, env.CHAT_KEY)) return bad(401, "INVALID_CHAT_KEY");
  return null;
};

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// VALIDATION
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
const validateChat = (body) => {
  const errors = [];
  if (!body || typeof body !== "object") errors.push("Body must be JSON object");
  if (!body?.message) errors.push("Missing message");
  if (!body?.session_id) errors.push("Missing session_id");
  if (body?.message && typeof body.message !== "string") errors.push("message must be string");
  if (body?.message && body.message.length > 5000) errors.push("message exceeds 5000 chars");
  return errors.length === 0 ? { valid: true } : { valid: false, errors };
};

const validateWhisper = (body) => {
  const errors = [];
  const required = ["type", "timestamp", "correlation_id", "actor_id", "payload"];
  for (const field of required) if (!body?.[field]) errors.push(`Missing ${field}`);
  if (body?.type && body.type !== "classroom_audio_capture_v1") errors.push("type must be classroom_audio_capture_v1");
  if (body?.actor_id && body.actor_id !== "AGENT-100") errors.push("actor_id must be AGENT-100");
  return errors.length === 0 ? { valid: true } : { valid: false, errors };
};

const validateMakeWebhook = (body) => {
  const errors = [];
  const required = ["name", "email", "phone", "note", "timestamp"];
  for (const field of required) if (!body?.[field]) errors.push(`Missing ${field}`);
  if (body?.email && !String(body.email).includes("@")) errors.push("Invalid email format");
  return errors.length === 0 ? { valid: true } : { valid: false, errors };
};

// DURABLE OBJECT: LEDGER
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
export class PhoenixLedger {
  constructor(state, env) {
    this.state = state;
    this.env = env;
    this.sql = state.storage.sql;
    this.initialized = false;
  }

  async init() {
    if (this.initialized) return;
    this.sql.exec(`
      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS blocks (
        ledgerid TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'GLOBAL',
        height INTEGER NOT NULL,
        blockid TEXT NOT NULL,
        kind TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'EVENT',
        agent TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'SOVEREIGN',
        nonce TEXT NOT NULL,
        payload_canon TEXT NOT NULL,
        payload_hash TEXT NOT NULL,
        prev_hash TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
        block_hash TEXT NOT NULL,
        created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
        PRIMARY KEY (ledgerid, height)
      );

      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS revenue_events (
        rev_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
        amount_vnd INTEGER NOT NULL,
        source TEXT NOT NULL,
        note_canon TEXT NOT NULL,
        block_hash TEXT NOT NULL,
        created_at TEXT NOT NULL
      );

      CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_blocks_hash ON blocks(block_hash);
      CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_revenue_block ON revenue_events(block_hash);
    `);
    this.initialized = true;
  }

  async wipeAll() {
    // Deterministic wipe
    this.sql.exec(`DELETE FROM revenue_events;`);
    this.sql.exec(`DELETE FROM blocks;`);
    // Optional compaction
    this.sql.exec(`VACUUM;`);
  }

  async fetch(request) {
    await this.init();
    const url = new URL(request.url);

    if (url.pathname === "/ledger/height") {
      const result = this.sql.exec("SELECT COALESCE(MAX(height), -1) as height FROM blocks").toArray();
      return new Response(JSON.stringify({
        ok: true,
        height: result[0].height,
        anchor: EMPIRE_CONFIG.ANCHOR_HEIGHT
      }), { status: 200, headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", "X-Gospel": "444", "X-Version": EMPIRE_CONFIG.VERSION } });
    }

    if (url.pathname === "/admin/wipe" && request.method === "POST") {
      // Worker already enforced sovereign auth before proxying here.
      return await this.state.blockConcurrencyWhile(async () => {
        await this.wipeAll();
        return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, wiped: true, timestamp: nowISO() }), {
          status: 200,
          headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", "X-Gospel": "444", "X-Version": EMPIRE_CONFIG.VERSION }
        });
      });
    }

    if (url.pathname === "/ledger/append" && request.method === "POST") {
      return await this.state.blockConcurrencyWhile(async () => {
        const body = await badBody(request);
        if (!body || typeof body !== "object") return bad(400, "INVALID_JSON", "Body must be JSON object");
        if (!body.payload || typeof body.payload !== "object") return bad(400, "INVALID_PAYLOAD", "payload must be JSON object");

        const last = this.sql.exec("SELECT height, block_hash FROM blocks ORDER BY height DESC LIMIT 1").toArray();
        const height = last.length > 0 ? last[0].height + 1 : 0;
        const prev_hash = last.length > 0 ? last[0].block_hash : "0".repeat(64);

        const nonce = crypto.randomUUID();
        const payload_canon = canonJSON(body.payload);
        const payload_hash = await sha256Hex(payload_canon);

        const blockData = {
          ledgerid: "GLOBAL",
          height,
          blockid: body.blockid || crypto.randomUUID(),
          kind: body.kind || "EVENT",
          agent: body.agent || "SOVEREIGN",
          nonce,
          payload_hash,
          prev_hash
        };

        const block_hash = await sha256Hex(canonJSON(blockData));

        this.sql.exec(
          `INSERT INTO blocks
           (ledgerid, height, blockid, kind, agent, nonce, payload_canon, payload_hash, prev_hash, block_hash, created_at)
           VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`,
          "GLOBAL",
          height,
          blockData.blockid,
          blockData.kind,
          blockData.agent,
          nonce,
          payload_canon,
          payload_hash,
          prev_hash,
          block_hash,
          nowISO()
        );

        if (blockData.kind === "REVENUE") {
          // Minimal guard
          const amt = body.payload.amount_vnd;
          const src = body.payload.source;
          if (!Number.isInteger(amt) || amt <= 0) return bad(400, "INVALID_REVENUE", "amount_vnd must be positive integer");
          if (!src || typeof src !== "string") return bad(400, "INVALID_REVENUE", "source must be string");

          const rev_id = crypto.randomUUID();
          this.sql.exec(
            `INSERT INTO revenue_events (rev_id, amount_vnd, source, note_canon, block_hash, created_at)
             VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`,
            rev_id,
            amt,
            src,
            canonJSON(body.payload.note || {}),
            block_hash,
            nowISO()
          );
        }

        return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, height, block_hash }), {
          status: 201,
          headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", "X-Gospel": "444", "X-Version": EMPIRE_CONFIG.VERSION }
        });
      });
    }

    if (url.pathname === "/ledger/verify") {
      const blocks = this.sql.exec("SELECT * FROM blocks ORDER BY height ASC").toArray();
      const issues = [];

      for (let i = 0; i < blocks.length; i++) {
        const b = blocks[i];

        // Height sequence
        if (b.height !== i) {
          issues.push({ height: b.height, issue: "height_sequence_broken", expected: i, actual: b.height });
        }

        // prev_hash linkage
        if (i === 0) {
          if (b.prev_hash !== "0".repeat(64)) {
            issues.push({ height: b.height, issue: "genesis_prev_hash_not_zero", expected: "0".repeat(64), actual: b.prev_hash });
          }
        } else {
          const prev = blocks[i - 1];
          if (b.prev_hash !== prev.block_hash) {
            issues.push({ height: b.height, issue: "prev_hash_mismatch", expected: prev.block_hash, actual: b.prev_hash });
          }
        }

        // Recompute payload_hash
        const recomputedPayloadHash = await sha256Hex(String(b.payload_canon));
        if (b.payload_hash !== recomputedPayloadHash) {
          issues.push({ height: b.height, issue: "payload_hash_mismatch", expected: recomputedPayloadHash, actual: b.payload_hash });
        }

        // Recompute block_hash deterministically from stored fields
        const recomputeBlockData = {
          ledgerid: b.ledgerid,
          height: b.height,
          blockid: b.blockid,
          kind: b.kind,
          agent: b.agent,
          nonce: b.nonce,
          payload_hash: b.payload_hash,
          prev_hash: b.prev_hash
        };
        const recomputedBlockHash = await sha256Hex(canonJSON(recomputeBlockData));
        if (b.block_hash !== recomputedBlockHash) {
          issues.push({ height: b.height, issue: "block_hash_mismatch", expected: recomputedBlockHash, actual: b.block_hash });
        }
      }

      return new Response(JSON.stringify({
        ok: issues.length === 0,
        height: blocks.length > 0 ? blocks[blocks.length - 1].height : -1,
        block_count: blocks.length,
        issues
      }), { status: 200, headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", "X-Gospel": "444", "X-Version": EMPIRE_CONFIG.VERSION } });
    }

    if (url.pathname === "/ledger/query") {
      const height = parseInt(url.searchParams.get("height"));
      if (isNaN(height)) return bad(400, "INVALID_HEIGHT", "height must be integer");

      const result = this.sql.exec("SELECT * FROM blocks WHERE height = ?", height).toArray();
      if (result.length === 0) return bad(404, "BLOCK_NOT_FOUND", `No block at height ${height}`);

      return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, block: result[0] }), {
        status: 200,
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", "X-Gospel": "444", "X-Version": EMPIRE_CONFIG.VERSION }
      });
    }

    if (url.pathname === "/revenue/total") {
      const result = this.sql.exec("SELECT COALESCE(SUM(amount_vnd), 0) as total FROM revenue_events").toArray();
      const total = result[0].total;
      const target = EMPIRE_CONFIG.TARGET_VND;
      const progress = target > 0 ? (total / target * 100).toFixed(2) : "0.00";

      return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, total_vnd: total, target_vnd: target, progress_percent: progress }), {
        status: 200,
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", "X-Gospel": "444", "X-Version": EMPIRE_CONFIG.VERSION }
      });
    }

    return bad(404, "LEDGER_ROUTE_NOT_FOUND");
  }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// DURABLE OBJECT: SESSIONS
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
export class PhoenixSessionStore {
  constructor(state, env) {
    this.state = state;
    this.env = env;
    this.sql = state.storage.sql;
    this.initialized = false;
  }

  async init() {
    if (this.initialized) return;
    this.sql.exec(`
      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS messages (
        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
        session_id TEXT NOT NULL,
        role TEXT NOT NULL,
        content TEXT NOT NULL,
        provider TEXT,
        intent TEXT,
        timestamp TEXT NOT NULL
      );
      CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_session ON messages(session_id);
    `);
    this.initialized = true;
  }

  async wipeAll() {
    this.sql.exec(`DELETE FROM messages;`);
    this.sql.exec(`VACUUM;`);
  }

  async fetch(request) {
    await this.init();
    const url = new URL(request.url);

    if (url.pathname === "/admin/wipe" && request.method === "POST") {
      return await this.state.blockConcurrencyWhile(async () => {
        await this.wipeAll();
        return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, wiped: true, timestamp: nowISO() }), {
          status: 200,
          headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", "X-Gospel": "444", "X-Version": EMPIRE_CONFIG.VERSION }
        });
      });
    }

    if (url.pathname === "/append" && request.method === "POST") {
      const body = await badBody(request);
      if (!body || typeof body !== "object") return bad(400, "INVALID_JSON", "Body must be JSON object");

      const { session_id, role, content, provider, intent } = body;
      if (!session_id || !role || !content) return bad(400, "MISSING_FIELDS", "session_id, role, content required");

      this.sql.exec(
        `INSERT INTO messages (session_id, role, content, provider, intent, timestamp)
         VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`,
        session_id,
        role,
        String(content),
        provider || null,
        intent || null,
        nowISO()
      );

      return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true }), {
        status: 200,
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", "X-Gospel": "444", "X-Version": EMPIRE_CONFIG.VERSION }
      });
    }

    if (url.pathname === "/history") {
      const sessionId = url.searchParams.get("session_id");
      if (!sessionId) return bad(400, "MISSING_SESSION_ID", "session_id query param required");

      const messages = this.sql.exec(
        "SELECT role, content, provider, intent, timestamp FROM messages WHERE session_id = ? ORDER BY id ASC",
        sessionId
      ).toArray();

      return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, session_id: sessionId, messages, count: messages.length }), {
        status: 200,
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", "X-Gospel": "444", "X-Version": EMPIRE_CONFIG.VERSION }
      });
    }

    return bad(404, "SESSION_ROUTE_NOT_FOUND");
  }
}
